Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in surfaces theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 39 and the invariant I(A,B) = 616 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 39 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the surfaces structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 616. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
